## Narration 1999

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A2006)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ali reporting Muhammad&#x27;s directions, with the source&#x27;s transmitter memory qualification. The complete encounter concerns burying the named relative, returning, washing and a supplication. The transmitted inability to remember the supplication remains part of the account; no missing prayer wording is supplied.</p>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ سُفْيَانَ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي أَبُو إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ نَاجِيَةَ بْنِ كَعْبٍ، عَنْ عَلِيٍّ، قَالَ قُلْتُ لِلنَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، إِنَّ عَمَّكَ الشَّيْخَ الضَّالَّ مَاتَ، فَمَنْ يُوَارِيهِ قَالَ ‏:‏ ‏ &quot;‏ اذْهَبْ فَوَارِ أَبَاكَ وَلاَ تُحْدِثَنَّ حَدَثًا حَتَّى تَأْتِيَنِي ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَوَارَيْتُهُ ثُمَّ جِئْتُ فَأَمَرَنِي فَاغْتَسَلْتُ وَدَعَا لِي، وَذَكَرَ دُعَاءً لَمْ أَحْفَظْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2006" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   2006 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ali reporting Muhammad&#x27;s directions, with the source&#x27;s transmitter memory qualification. The complete encounter concerns burying the named relative, returning, washing and a supplication. The transmitted inability to remember the supplication remains part of the account; no missing prayer wording is supplied.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that &#x27;Ali said: &quot;I said to the Prophet: &#x27;Your paternal uncle, the old misguided man has died. Who will bury him?&#x27; He said: &#x27;Go and bury your father, then do not do anything until you come to me.&#x27; So I buried him then I came, and he told me to perform Ghusl and he prayed for me, and he mentioned a supplication that I do not remember</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2006" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ali reporting Muhammad&#x27;s directions, with the source&#x27;s transmitter memory qualification. The complete encounter concerns burying the named relative, returning, washing and a supplication. The transmitted inability to remember the supplication remains part of the account; no missing prayer wording is supplied.</p>

<p dir="auto">अली (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के मैंने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा: आपके बुढ़े गुम-कर्दा-ए-राह चाचा (अबू तालिब) मर गये हैं, उन्हें कौन दफ़न करे? आपने फ़रमाया: तुम जाओ और अपने बाप को दफ़न कर दो और कोई नयी चीज़ न करना जब तक मेरे पास लौट न आना, चुनांचे मैं उन्हें दफ़न कर आया, तो आपने मेरे लिए (नहाने का) हुक्म दिया, मैंने ग़ुस्ल किया, और आपने मुझे दुआ दी। रावी नाजिया बिन क&#x27;ब कहते हैं: और अली (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने एक ऐसी दुआ का ज़िक्र किया जिसे मैं याद नहीं रख सका।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2006" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ali reporting Muhammad&#x27;s directions, with the source&#x27;s transmitter memory qualification. The complete encounter concerns burying the named relative, returning, washing and a supplication. The transmitted inability to remember the supplication remains part of the account; no missing prayer wording is supplied.</p>

<p dir="rtl">علی رضی الله عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا: آپ کے بوڑھے گم کردہ راہ چچا ( ابوطالب ) مر گئے ہیں، انہیں کون دفن کرے؟ آپ نے فرمایا: ”تم جاؤ اور اپنے باپ کو دفن کر دو اور کوئی نئی چیز نہ کرنا جب تک میرے پاس لوٹ نہ آنا“، چنانچہ میں انہیں دفن کر آیا، تو آپ نے میرے لیے ( نہانے کا ) حکم دیا، میں نے غسل کیا، اور آپ نے مجھے دعا دی۔ راوی ناجیہ بن کعب کہتے ہیں: اور علی رضی اللہ عنہ نے ایک ایسی دعا کا ذکر کیا جسے میں یاد نہیں رکھ سکا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2006" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ali reporting Muhammad&#x27;s directions, with the source&#x27;s transmitter memory qualification. The complete encounter concerns burying the named relative, returning, washing and a supplication. The transmitted inability to remember the supplication remains part of the account; no missing prayer wording is supplied.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke maine Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha: Aapke budhe gum-karda-e-raah chacha (Abu Talib) mar gaye hain, unhein kaun dafan kare? Aapne farmaya: Tum jao aur apne baap ko dafan kar do aur koi nayi cheez na karna jab tak mere paas laut na aana, chunancha main unhein dafan kar aaya, toh aapne mere liye (nahaane ka) hukm diya, maine ghusl kiya, aur aapne mujhe dua di. Rawi Najiya bin Kab kehte hain: Aur Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ek aisi dua ka zikr kiya jise main yaad nahi rakh saka.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2006]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2006" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 2000

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A2007)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Sad, giving his own burial instruction and citing Muhammad&#x27;s burial. The source quotes Sad&#x27;s request for his burial arrangements and an explicit comparison with the Messenger&#x27;s burial. Sad remains the speaker; the request is not transformed into a command spoken by Muhammad.</p>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا عَمْرُو بْنُ عَلِيٍّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ سَعْدٍ، قَالَ ‏:‏ الْحَدُوا لِي لَحْدًا، وَانْصِبُوا عَلَىَّ نَصْبًا كَمَا فُعِلَ بِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2007]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/2007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Sad, giving his own burial instruction and citing Muhammad&#x27;s burial. The source quotes Sad&#x27;s request for his burial arrangements and an explicit comparison with the Messenger&#x27;s burial. Sad remains the speaker; the request is not transformed into a command spoken by Muhammad.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Sa&#x27;d said: &quot;Make a niche for me in the side of the grave and set up (bricks) over me as was done for the Messenger of Allah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2007]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Sad, giving his own burial instruction and citing Muhammad&#x27;s burial. The source quotes Sad&#x27;s request for his burial arrangements and an explicit comparison with the Messenger&#x27;s burial. Sad remains the speaker; the request is not transformed into a command spoken by Muhammad. The Hindi source is abbreviated and omits the brick instruction present in other fields. Its own supplied wording is retained without supplementation.</p>

<p dir="auto">साद बिन आबि वक़्क़ास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि मेरे लिए बाघलि क़ब्र खोदना रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के लिए की गयी थी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 2007]</p>
